var DoubleLinkedListImplementation = function() {
    this.head = null;
    this.tail = null;
    this.size = 0;
    this.Node = function(value) {
        this.value = value;
        this.next = null;
        this.prev = null;
    };
    this.append = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.head) { this.head = node; this.tail = node; }
        else { node.prev = this.tail; this.tail.next = node; this.tail = node; }
        this.size++;
    };
    this.prepend = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.head) { this.head = node; this.tail = node; }
        else { node.next = this.head; this.head.prev = node; this.head = node; }
        this.size++;
    };
    this.get = function(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return null;
        var current = index < this.size / 2 ? this.head : this.tail;
        if (index < this.size / 2) {
            for (var i = 0; i < index; i++) current = current.next;
        } else {
            for (var i = this.size - 1; i > index; i--) current = current.prev;
        }
        return current.value;
    };
    this.set = function(index, value) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return false;
        var current = index < this.size / 2 ? this.head : this.tail;
        if (index < this.size / 2) {
            for (var i = 0; i < index; i++) current = current.next;
        } else {
            for (var i = this.size - 1; i > index; i--) current = current.prev;
        }
        current.value = value;
        return true;
    };
    this.insert = function(index, value) {
        if (index < 0 || index > this.size) return false;
        if (index === 0) { this.prepend(value); return true; }
        if (index === this.size) { this.append(value); return true; }
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        var prev = index < this.size / 2 ? this.head : this.tail;
        if (index < this.size / 2) {
            for (var i = 0; i < index - 1; i++) prev = prev.next;
        } else {
            for (var i = this.size - 1; i > index - 1; i--) prev = prev.prev;
        }
        node.next = prev.next;
        node.prev = prev;
        prev.next.prev = node;
        prev.next = node;
        this.size++;
        return true;
    };
    this.remove = function(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return null;
        var current = index < this.size / 2 ? this.head : this.tail;
        if (index < this.size / 2) {
            for (var i = 0; i < index; i++) current = current.next;
        } else {
            for (var i = this.size - 1; i > index; i--) current = current.prev;
        }
        if (current.prev) current.prev.next = current.next;
        else this.head = current.next;
        if (current.next) current.next.prev = current.prev;
        else this.tail = current.prev;
        this.size--;
        return current.value;
    };
    this.toArray = function() {
        var result = [];
        var current = this.head;
        while (current) { result.push(current.value); current = current.next; }
        return result;
    };
    this.reverse = function() {
        var current = this.head;
        var temp = null;
        while (current) {
            temp = current.prev;
            current.prev = current.next;
            current.next = temp;
            current = current.prev;
        }
        temp = this.head;
        this.head = this.tail;
        this.tail = temp;
    };
    this.size = function() { return this.size; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.size === 0; };
    this.clear = function() { this.head = null; this.tail = null; this.size = 0; };
};
var CircularLinkedList = function() {
    this.head = null;
    this.size = 0;
    this.Node = function(value) {
        this.value = value;
        this.next = null;
    };
    this.append = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.head) { node.next = node; this.head = node; }
        else {
            var current = this.head;
            while (current.next !== this.head) current = current.next;
            node.next = this.head;
            current.next = node;
        }
        this.size++;
    };
    this.prepend = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.head) { node.next = node; this.head = node; }
        else {
            var current = this.head;
            while (current.next !== this.head) current = current.next;
            node.next = this.head;
            current.next = node;
            this.head = node;
        }
        this.size++;
    };
    this.get = function(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return null;
        var current = this.head;
        for (var i = 0; i < index; i++) current = current.next;
        return current.value;
    };
    this.remove = function(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return null;
        var current = this.head;
        if (this.size === 1) { this.head = null; this.size--; return current.value; }
        for (var i = 0; i < index - 1; i++) current = current.next;
        var toRemove = current.next;
        current.next = toRemove.next;
        if (index === 0) this.head = toRemove.next;
        this.size--;
        return toRemove.value;
    };
    this.toArray = function() {
        var result = [];
        var current = this.head;
        if (!current) return result;
        do {
            result.push(current.value);
            current = current.next;
        } while (current !== this.head);
        return result;
    };
};
var StackImplementation = function() {
    this.items = [];
    this.push = function(item) { this.items.push(item); };
    this.pop = function() { return this.items.pop(); };
    this.peek = function() { return this.items[this.items.length - 1]; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.items.length === 0; };
    this.size = function() { return this.items.length; };
    this.clear = function() { this.items = []; };
    this.toArray = function() { return this.items.slice(); };
    this.contains = function(item) { return this.items.indexOf(item) >= 0; };
    this.sort = function(compareFn) { this.items.sort(compareFn); };
    this.reverse = function() { this.items.reverse(); };
};
var QueueImplementation = function() {
    this.items = [];
    this.enqueue = function(item) { this.items.push(item); };
    this.dequeue = function() { return this.items.shift(); };
    this.front = function() { return this.items[0]; };
    this.rear = function() { return this.items[this.items.length - 1]; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.items.length === 0; };
    this.size = function() { return this.items.length; };
    this.clear = function() { this.items = []; };
    this.toArray = function() { return this.items.slice(); };
    this.contains = function(item) { return this.items.indexOf(item) >= 0; };
    this.peekAt = function(index) { return this.items[index]; };
};

var DequeImplementation = function() {
    this.items = [];
    this.addFront = function(item) { this.items.unshift(item); };
    this.addRear = function(item) { this.items.push(item); };
    this.removeFront = function() { return this.items.shift(); };
    this.removeRear = function() { return this.items.pop(); };
    this.front = function() { return this.items[0]; };
    this.rear = function() { return this.items[this.items.length - 1]; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.items.length === 0; };
    this.size = function() { return this.items.length; };
    this.clear = function() { this.items = []; };
    this.toArray = function() { return this.items.slice(); };
};
var PriorityQueueNode = function(value, priority) {
    this.value = value;
    this.priority = priority;
};
var PriorityQueueImplementation = function() {
    this.items = [];
    this.enqueue = function(value, priority) {
        priority = priority || 0;
        var node = new PriorityQueueNode(value, priority);
        var added = false;
        for (var i = 0; i < this.items.length; i++) {
            if (priority < this.items[i].priority) {
                this.items.splice(i, 0, node);
                added = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!added) this.items.push(node);
    };
    this.dequeue = function() { return this.items.shift().value; };
    this.front = function() { return this.items[0].value; };
    this.rear = function() { return this.items[this.items.length - 1].value; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.items.length === 0; };
    this.size = function() { return this.items.length; };
    this.clear = function() { this.items = []; };
    this.toArray = function() { return this.items.map(function(n) { return n.value; }); };
};
var CircularBuffer = function(capacity) {
    this.capacity = capacity || 10;
    this.buffer = new Array(this.capacity);
    this.head = 0;
    this.tail = 0;
    this.size = 0;
    this.write = function(item) {
        this.buffer[this.tail] = item;
        this.tail = (this.tail + 1) % this.capacity;
        if (this.size < this.capacity) this.size++;
        else this.head = (this.head + 1) % this.capacity;
    };
    this.read = function() {
        if (this.size === 0) return null;
        var item = this.buffer[this.head];
        this.head = (this.head + 1) % this.capacity;
        this.size--;
        return item;
    };
    this.peek = function() { return this.size > 0 ? this.buffer[this.head] : null; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.size === 0; };
    this.isFull = function() { return this.size === this.capacity; };
    this.available = function() { return this.capacity - this.size; };
    this.clear = function() { this.head = 0; this.tail = 0; this.size = 0; };
};
var BinaryHeapImplementation = function(comparator) {
    this.items = [];
    this.comparator = comparator || function(a, b) { return a - b; };
    this.parent = function(index) { return Math.floor((index - 1) / 2); };
    this.leftChild = function(index) { return 2 * index + 1; };
    this.rightChild = function(index) { return 2 * index + 2; };
    this.swap = function(i, j) {
        var temp = this.items[i];
        this.items[i] = this.items[j];
        this.items[j] = temp;
    };
    this.bubbleUp = function(index) {
        while (index > 0) {
            var p = this.parent(index);
            if (this.comparator(this.items[index], this.items[p]) < 0) {
                this.swap(index, p);
                index = p;
            } else break;
        }
    };
    this.bubbleDown = function(index) {
        while (true) {
            var smallest = index;
            var left = this.leftChild(index);
            var right = this.rightChild(index);
            if (left < this.items.length && this.comparator(this.items[left], this.items[smallest]) < 0) smallest = left;
            if (right < this.items.length && this.comparator(this.items[right], this.items[smallest]) < 0) smallest = right;
            if (smallest !== index) { this.swap(index, smallest); index = smallest; }
            else break;
        }
    };
    this.insert = function(item) {
        this.items.push(item);
        this.bubbleUp(this.items.length - 1);
    };
    this.extract = function() {
        if (this.items.length === 0) return null;
        var min = this.items[0];
        var last = this.items.pop();
        if (this.items.length > 0) {
            this.items[0] = last;
            this.bubbleDown(0);
        }
        return min;
    };
    this.peek = function() { return this.items.length > 0 ? this.items[0] : null; };
    this.size = function() { return this.items.length; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.items.length === 0; };
    this.clear = function() { this.items = []; };
};
var GraphImplementation = function(directed) {
    this.directed = directed || false;
    this.vertices = {};
    this.addVertex = function(key) {
        if (!this.vertices[key]) this.vertices[key] = { edges: {} };
    };
    this.removeVertex = function(key) {
        if (!this.vertices[key]) return;
        delete this.vertices[key];
        for (var v in this.vertices) {
            delete this.vertices[v].edges[key];
        }
    };
    this.addEdge = function(from, to, weight) {
        if (!this.vertices[from]) this.addVertex(from);
        if (!this.vertices[to]) this.addVertex(to);
        this.vertices[from].edges[to] = weight || 1;
        if (!this.directed) this.vertices[to].edges[from] = weight || 1;
    };
    this.removeEdge = function(from, to) {
        if (this.vertices[from]) delete this.vertices[from].edges[to];
        if (!this.directed && this.vertices[to]) delete this.vertices[to].edges[from];
    };
    this.getNeighbors = function(key) {
        if (!this.vertices[key]) return [];
        var neighbors = [];
        for (var neighbor in this.vertices[key].edges) neighbors.push(neighbor);
        return neighbors;
    };
    this.hasPath = function(from, to) {
        var visited = {};
        var queue = [from];
        visited[from] = true;
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            var current = queue.shift();
            if (current === to) return true;
            var neighbors = this.getNeighbors(current);
            for (var i = 0; i < neighbors.length; i++) {
                if (!visited[neighbors[i]]) {
                    visited[neighbors[i]] = true;
                    queue.push(neighbors[i]);
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.getPath = function(from, to) {
        var visited = {};
        var parent = {};
        var queue = [from];
        visited[from] = true;
        parent[from] = null;
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            var current = queue.shift();
            if (current === to) {
                var path = [];
                var node = to;
                while (node !== null) { path.unshift(node); node = parent[node]; }
                return path;
            }
            var neighbors = this.getNeighbors(current);
            for (var i = 0; i < neighbors.length; i++) {
                if (!visited[neighbors[i]]) {
                    visited[neighbors[i]] = true;
                    parent[neighbors[i]] = current;
                    queue.push(neighbors[i]);
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    };
    this.getAllPaths = function(from, to) {
        var paths = [];
        var visited = {};
        var currentPath = [from];
        visited[from] = true;
        function dfs(current) {
            if (current === to) { paths.push(currentPath.slice()); return; }
            var neighbors = this.getNeighbors(current);
            for (var i = 0; i < neighbors.length; i++) {
                if (!visited[neighbors[i]]) {
                    visited[neighbors[i]] = true;
                    currentPath.push(neighbors[i]);
                    dfs(neighbors[i]);
                    currentPath.pop();
                    visited[neighbors[i]] = false;
                }
            }
        }
        dfs.call(this, from);
        return paths;
    };
};
var TreeNode = function(value) {
    this.value = value;
    this.children = [];
    this.addChild = function(node) { this.children.push(node); };
    this.removeChild = function(node) {
        var index = this.children.indexOf(node);
        if (index >= 0) this.children.splice(index, 1);
    };
};
var TreeImplementation = function(value) {
    this.root = value ? new TreeNode(value) : null;
    this.traverseDF = function(callback) {
        function visit(node) {
            callback(node);
            for (var i = 0; i < node.children.length; i++) visit(node.children[i]);
        }
        if (this.root) visit(this.root);
    };
    this.traverseBFS = function(callback) {
        var queue = [this.root];
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            var node = queue.shift();
            callback(node);
            for (var i = 0; i < node.children.length; i++) queue.push(node.children[i]);
        }
    };
    this.contains = function(value) {
        var found = false;
        this.traverseDF(function(node) { if (node.value === value) found = true; });
        return found;
    };
    this.findNode = function(value) {
        var result = null;
        this.traverseDF(function(node) { if (node.value === value) result = node; });
        return result;
    };
    this.height = function() {
        var maxHeight = 0;
        function computeHeight(node, depth) {
            maxHeight = Math.max(maxHeight, depth);
            for (var i = 0; i < node.children.length; i++) computeHeight(node.children[i], depth + 1);
        }
        if (this.root) computeHeight(this.root, 1);
        return maxHeight;
    };
};
var BinarySearchTreeImplementation = function() {
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function(value) {
        this.value = value;
        this.left = null;
        this.right = null;
    };
    this.insert = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.root) { this.root = node; return; }
        var current = this.root;
        while (true) {
            if (value < current.value) {
                if (!current.left) { current.left = node; break; }
                current = current.left;
            } else {
                if (!current.right) { current.right = node; break; }
                current = current.right;
            }
        }
    };
    this.search = function(value) {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            if (value === current.value) return true;
            if (value < current.value) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.findMin = function() {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current && current.left) current = current.left;
        return current ? current.value : null;
    };
    this.findMax = function() {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current && current.right) current = current.right;
        return current ? current.value : null;
    };
    this.remove = function(value) {
        this.root = this.removeNode(this.root, value);
    };
    this.removeNode = function(node, value) {
        if (!node) return null;
        if (value < node.value) {
            node.left = this.removeNode(node.left, value);
        } else if (value > node.value) {
            node.right = this.removeNode(node.right, value);
        } else {
            if (!node.left) return node.right;
            if (!node.right) return node.left;
            var minRight = node.right;
            while (minRight.left) minRight = minRight.left;
            node.value = minRight.value;
            node.right = this.removeNode(node.right, minRight.value);
        }
        return node;
    };
    this.inOrder = function(callback) {
        this.inOrderTraversal(this.root, callback);
    };
    this.inOrderTraversal = function(node, callback) {
        if (node) {
            this.inOrderTraversal(node.left, callback);
            callback(node.value);
            this.inOrderTraversal(node.right, callback);
        }
    };
    this.preOrder = function(callback) {
        this.preOrderTraversal(this.root, callback);
    };
    this.preOrderTraversal = function(node, callback) {
        if (node) {
            callback(node.value);
            this.preOrderTraversal(node.left, callback);
            this.preOrderTraversal(node.right, callback);
        }
    };
    this.postOrder = function(callback) {
        this.postOrderTraversal(this.root, callback);
    };
    this.postOrderTraversal = function(node, callback) {
        if (node) {
            this.postOrderTraversal(node.left, callback);
            this.postOrderTraversal(node.right, callback);
            callback(node.value);
        }
    };
    this.height = function() { return this.calcHeight(this.root); };
    this.calcHeight = function(node) {
        if (!node) return 0;
        var leftHeight = this.calcHeight(node.left);
        var rightHeight = this.calcHeight(node.right);
        return Math.max(leftHeight, rightHeight) + 1;
    };
    this.isBalanced = function() { return this.checkBalanced(this.root).balanced; };
    this.checkBalanced = function(node) {
        if (!node) return { height: 0, balanced: true };
        var left = this.checkBalanced(node.left);
        var right = this.checkBalanced(node.right);
        var balanced = left.balanced && right.balanced && Math.abs(left.height - right.height) <= 1;
        return { height: Math.max(left.height, right.height) + 1, balanced: balanced };
    };
};
var RedBlackTreeImplementation = function() {
    this.RED = true;
    this.BLACK = false;
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function(value) {
        this.value = value;
        this.color = this.RED;
        this.left = null;
        this.right = null;
        this.parent = null;
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(node) {
        var right = node.right;
        node.right = right.left;
        if (right.left) right.left.parent = node;
        right.parent = node.parent;
        if (!node.parent) this.root = right;
        else if (node === node.parent.left) node.parent.left = right;
        else node.parent.right = right;
        right.left = node;
        node.parent = right;
    };
    this.rotateRight = function(node) {
        var left = node.left;
        node.left = left.right;
        if (left.right) left.right.parent = node;
        left.parent = node.parent;
        if (!node.parent) this.root = left;
        else if (node === node.parent.right) node.parent.right = left;
        else node.parent.left = left;
        left.right = node;
        node.parent = left;
    };
    this.insert = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        var parent = null;
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            parent = current;
            if (node.value < current.value) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        node.parent = parent;
        if (!parent) this.root = node;
        else if (node.value < parent.value) parent.left = node;
        else parent.right = node;
        this.insertFixup(node);
    };
    this.insertFixup = function(node) {
        while (node.parent && node.parent.color === this.RED) {
            if (node.parent === node.parent.parent.left) {
                var uncle = node.parent.parent.right;
                if (uncle && uncle.color === this.RED) {
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    uncle.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    node = node.parent.parent;
                } else {
                    if (node === node.parent.right) { node = node.parent; this.rotateLeft(node); }
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    this.rotateRight(node.parent.parent);
                }
            } else {
                var uncle = node.parent.parent.left;
                if (uncle && uncle.color === this.RED) {
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    uncle.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    node = node.parent.parent;
                } else {
                    if (node === node.parent.left) { node = node.parent; this.rotateRight(node); }
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    this.rotateLeft(node.parent.parent);
                }
            }
        }
        this.root.color = this.BLACK;
    };
    this.search = function(value) {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            if (value === current.value) return true;
            if (value < current.value) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.inOrder = function(callback) {
        this.inOrderTraversal(this.root, callback);
    };
    this.inOrderTraversal = function(node, callback) {
        if (node) {
            this.inOrderTraversal(node.left, callback);
            callback(node.value);
            this.inOrderTraversal(node.right, callback);
        }
    };
};
var BTreeImplementation = function(order) {
    this.order = order || 3;
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function() {
        this.keys = [];
        this.children = [];
        this.leaf = true;
    };
    this.splitChild = function(parent, index, child) {
        var newChild = new this.Node();
        newChild.leaf = child.leaf;
        for (var j = 0; j < this.order - 1; j++) newChild.keys.push(child.keys[j + this.order]);
        if (!child.leaf) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.order; j++) newChild.children.push(child.children[j + this.order]);
        }
        child.keys = child.keys.slice(0, this.order - 1);
        for (var j = parent.keys.length; j >= index + 1; j--) parent.children[j + 1] = parent.children[j];
        parent.children[index + 1] = newChild;
        for (var j = parent.keys.length - 1; j >= index; j--) parent.keys[j + 1] = parent.keys[j];
        parent.keys[index] = child.keys[this.order - 1];
        child.keys = child.keys.slice(0, this.order - 1);
    };
    this.insert = function(value) {
        if (!this.root) this.root = new this.Node();
        if (this.root.keys.length === 2 * this.order - 1) {
            var newRoot = new this.Node();
            newRoot.leaf = false;
            newRoot.children.push(this.root);
            this.splitChild(newRoot, 0, this.root);
            this.root = newRoot;
        }
        this.insertNonFull(this.root, value);
    };
    this.insertNonFull = function(node, value) {
        var i = node.keys.length - 1;
        if (node.leaf) {
            while (i >= 0 && value < node.keys[i]) i--;
            node.keys.splice(i + 1, 0, value);
        } else {
            while (i >= 0 && value < node.keys[i]) i--;
            i++;
            if (node.children[i].keys.length === 2 * this.order - 1) {
                this.splitChild(node, i, node.children[i]);
                if (value > node.keys[i]) i++;
            }
            this.insertNonFull(node.children[i], value);
        }
    };
    this.search = function(value) {
        return this.searchNode(this.root, value);
    };
    this.searchNode = function(node, value) {
        var i = 0;
        while (i < node.keys.length && value > node.keys[i]) i++;
        if (i < node.keys.length && value === node.keys[i]) return true;
        if (node.leaf) return false;
        return this.searchNode(node.children[i], value);
    };
};
var TrieImplementation = function() {
    this.root = { children: {}, isEnd: false };
    this.insert = function(word) {
        var current = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!current.children[char]) current.children[char] = { children: {}, isEnd: false };
            current = current.children[char];
        }
        current.isEnd = true;
    };
    this.search = function(word) {
        var current = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!current.children[char]) return false;
            current = current.children[char];
        }
        return current.isEnd;
    };
    this.startsWith = function(prefix) {
        var current = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
            var char = prefix[i];
            if (!current.children[char]) return false;
            current = current.children[char];
        }
        return true;
    };
    this.getAllWords = function() {
        var words = [];
        this.getWords(this.root, "", words);
        return words;
    };
    this.getWords = function(node, prefix, words) {
        if (node.isEnd) words.push(prefix);
        for (var char in node.children) {
            this.getWords(node.children[char], prefix + char, words);
        }
    };
};
var GraphBFS = function() {
    this.graph = {};
    this.addVertex = function(v) { if (!this.graph[v]) this.graph[v] = []; };
    this.addEdge = function(v1, v2) {
        if (!this.graph[v1]) this.graph[v1] = [];
        if (!this.graph[v2]) this.graph[v2] = [];
        this.graph[v1].push(v2);
        this.graph[v2].push(v1);
    };
    this.bfs = function(start) {
        var visited = {};
        var queue = [start];
        var result = [];
        visited[start] = true;
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            var v = queue.shift();
            result.push(v);
            for (var i = 0; i < this.graph[v].length; i++) {
                var neighbor = this.graph[v][i];
                if (!visited[neighbor]) {
                    visited[neighbor] = true;
                    queue.push(neighbor);
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.dfs = function(start) {
        var visited = {};
        var result = [];
        this.dfsHelper(start, visited, result);
        return result;
    };
    this.dfsHelper = function(v, visited, result) {
        visited[v] = true;
        result.push(v);
        for (var i = 0; i < this.graph[v].length; i++) {
            var neighbor = this.graph[v][i];
            if (!visited[neighbor]) this.dfsHelper(neighbor, visited, result);
        }
    };
    this.hasCycle = function() {
        var visited = {};
        for (var v in this.graph) {
            if (!visited[v] && this.hasCycleHelper(v, visited, null)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.hasCycleHelper = function(v, visited, parent) {
        visited[v] = true;
        for (var i = 0; i < this.graph[v].length; i++) {
            var neighbor = this.graph[v][i];
            if (!visited[neighbor]) {
                if (this.hasCycleHelper(neighbor, visited, v)) return true;
            } else if (neighbor !== parent) return true;
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.shortestPath = function(start, end) {
        var visited = {};
        var parent = {};
        var queue = [start];
        visited[start] = true;
        parent[start] = null;
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            var v = queue.shift();
            if (v === end) {
                var path = [];
                var current = end;
                while (current !== null) { path.unshift(current); current = parent[current]; }
                return path;
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < this.graph[v].length; i++) {
                var neighbor = this.graph[v][i];
                if (!visited[neighbor]) {
                    visited[neighbor] = true;
                    parent[neighbor] = v;
                    queue.push(neighbor);
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    };
};
var DisjointSetImplementation = function() {
    this.parent = {};
    this.rank = {};
    this.makeSet = function(x) {
        if (!this.parent.hasOwnProperty(x)) {
            this.parent[x] = x;
            this.rank[x] = 0;
        }
    };
    this.find = function(x) {
        if (this.parent[x] !== x) this.parent[x] = this.find(this.parent[x]);
        return this.parent[x];
    };
    this.union = function(x, y) {
        var rootX = this.find(x);
        var rootY = this.find(y);
        if (rootX === rootY) return;
        if (this.rank[rootX] < this.rank[rootY]) this.parent[rootX] = rootY;
        else if (this.rank[rootX] > this.rank[rootY]) this.parent[rootY] = rootX;
        else { this.parent[rootY] = rootX; this.rank[rootX]++; }
    };
    this.connected = function(x, y) { return this.find(x) === this.find(y); };
};
var AdjacencyMatrix = function(numVertices) {
    this.numVertices = numVertices;
    this.matrix = [];
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < this.numVertices; i++) {
            this.matrix.push(new Array(this.numVertices).fill(0));
        }
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.addEdge = function(i, j, weight) {
        weight = weight || 1;
        this.matrix[i][j] = weight;
        this.matrix[j][i] = weight;
    };
    this.removeEdge = function(i, j) {
        this.matrix[i][j] = 0;
        this.matrix[j][i] = 0;
    };
    this.hasEdge = function(i, j) { return this.matrix[i][j] !== 0; };
    this.getEdge = function(i, j) { return this.matrix[i][j]; };
    this.getNeighbors = function(i) {
        var neighbors = [];
        for (var j = 0; j < this.numVertices; j++) {
            if (this.matrix[i][j] !== 0) neighbors.push(j);
        }
        return neighbors;
    };
};
var AdjacencyList = function() {
    this.list = {};
    this.addVertex = function(v) { if (!this.list[v]) this.list[v] = []; };
    this.addEdge = function(v1, v2, weight) {
        if (!this.list[v1]) this.list[v1] = [];
        if (!this.list[v2]) this.list[v2] = [];
        this.list[v1].push({ to: v2, weight: weight || 1 });
        this.list[v2].push({ to: v1, weight: weight || 1 });
    };
    this.removeEdge = function(v1, v2) {
        if (!this.list[v1]) return;
        this.list[v1] = this.list[v1].filter(function(e) { return e.to !== v2; });
        if (!this.list[v2]) return;
        this.list[v2] = this.list[v2].filter(function(e) { return e.to !== v1; });
    };
    this.getNeighbors = function(v) {
        if (!this.list[v]) return [];
        return this.list[v].map(function(e) { return e.to; });
    };
    this.hasEdge = function(v1, v2) {
        if (!this.list[v1]) return false;
        return this.list[v1].some(function(e) { return e.to === v2; });
    };
};
var GraphDijkstra = function() {
    this.graph = {};
    this.addVertex = function(v) { this.graph[v] = {}; };
    this.addEdge = function(v1, v2, weight) {
        if (!this.graph[v1]) this.graph[v1] = {};
        if (!this.graph[v2]) this.graph[v2] = {};
        this.graph[v1][v2] = weight;
        this.graph[v2][v1] = weight;
    };
    this.dijkstra = function(start, end) {
        var distances = {};
        var previous = {};
        var pq = [];
        for (var v in this.graph) {
            distances[v] = v === start ? 0 : Infinity;
            pq.push({ vertex: v, distance: distances[v] });
        }
        pq.sort(function(a, b) { return a.distance - b.distance; });
        while (pq.length > 0) {
            var current = pq.shift().vertex;
            if (current === end) break;
            for (var neighbor in this.graph[current]) {
                var alt = distances[current] + this.graph[current][neighbor];
                if (alt < distances[neighbor]) {
                    distances[neighbor] = alt;
                    previous[neighbor] = current;
                    for (var i = 0; i < pq.length; i++) {
                        if (pq[i].vertex === neighbor) { pq[i].distance = alt; break; }
                    }
                    pq.sort(function(a, b) { return a.distance - b.distance; });
                }
            }
        }
        var path = [];
        var current = end;
        while (current) { path.unshift(current); current = previous[current]; }
        return { path: path, distance: distances[end] };
    };
};
var GraphFloydWarshall = function(vertices) {
    this.vertices = vertices || [];
    this.dist = [];
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < this.vertices.length; i++) {
            this.dist[i] = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < this.vertices.length; j++) {
                if (i === j) this.dist[i][j] = 0;
                else this.dist[i][j] = Infinity;
            }
        }
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.addEdge = function(i, j, weight) {
        weight = weight || 1;
        this.dist[i][j] = weight;
        this.dist[j][i] = weight;
    };
    this.floydWarshall = function() {
        for (var k = 0; k < this.vertices.length; k++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < this.vertices.length; i++) {
                for (var j = 0; j < this.vertices.length; j++) {
                    if (this.dist[i][k] + this.dist[k][j] < this.dist[i][j]) {
                        this.dist[i][j] = this.dist[i][k] + this.dist[k][j];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    };
    this.getDistance = function(i, j) { return this.dist[i][j]; };
};
var OracleDatabaseConnector = function(config) {
    this.config = config || {};
    this.connection = null;
    this.connected = false;
    this.transactionCount = 0;
    this.statementCount = 0;
    this.maxStatements = 100;
    this.poolSize = config.poolSize || 10;
    this.pool = [];
    this.availableConnections = [];
    this.usedConnections = [];
    this.connect = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            self.connection = {
                id: Date.now(),
                host: self.config.host || "localhost",
                port: self.config.port || 1521,
                database: self.config.database || "ORCL",
                serviceName: self.config.serviceName || "orcl.oracle.com",
                user: self.config.user || "SYSTEM",
                sessionId: "Sess_" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15),
                serverVersion: "Oracle Database 19c Enterprise Edition Release 19.0.0.0.0",
                clientVersion: "Oracle JDBC Driver 19.14.0.0",
                sessionTimeout: 3600,
                lastActivity: Date.now(),
                isolationLevel: "READ_COMMITTED",
                prefetchRows: 100,
                fetchSize: 100,
                connectionPool: {
                    minPoolSize: 5,
                    maxPoolSize: self.poolSize,
                    poolIncrement: 1,
                    inactiveConnectionTimeout: 300
                }
            };
            self.connected = true;
            if (callback) callback(null, self.connection);
        }, 10);
    };
    this.disconnect = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            self.connection = null;
            self.connected = false;
            if (callback) callback(null);
        }, 10);
    };
    this.executeQuery = function(sql, params, callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        this.statementCount++;
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                sql: sql,
                params: params || [],
                rowsAffected: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 1,
                rows: [],
                metadata: {
                    columnCount: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 1,
                    columns: [],
                    statementId: "Stmt_" + self.statementCount,
                    statementType: sql.trim().toUpperCase().split(" ")[0],
                    executionTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 50,
                    fetchTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10,
                    parseTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 5
                },
                cursor: {
                    currentPosition: 0,
                    totalRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10,
                    isClosed: false
                },
                executionPlan: {
                    operation: "SELECT",
                    objectName: self.config.database + ".TABLE",
                    cost: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
                    cardinality: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000,
                    bytes: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) + 100000
                },
                sessionInfo: {
                    sessionId: self.connection.sessionId,
                    serialNumber: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1,
                    processId: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000,
                    program: "JDBC",
                    machine: require("os").hostname(),
                    terminal: "unknown"
                }
            };
            for (var i = 0; i < result.metadata.columns.length; i++) {
                result.metadata.columns.push({
                    name: "COLUMN_" + (i + 1),
                    type: ["VARCHAR2", "NUMBER", "DATE", "CLOB", "BLOB"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 5)],
                    displaySize: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10,
                    precision: Math.floor(Math.random() * 38),
                    scale: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10),
                    nullable: Math.random() > 0.5
                });
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < result.cursor.totalRows; i++) {
                var row = {};
                for (var j = 0; j < result.metadata.columns.length; j++) {
                    row[result.metadata.columns[j].name] = "Value_" + i + "_" + j;
                }
                result.rows.push(row);
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 50);
    };
    this.executeUpdate = function(sql, params, callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        this.statementCount++;
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                sql: sql,
                params: params || [],
                rowsAffected: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 1,
                returnParam: null,
                generatedKeys: [],
                statementId: "Stmt_" + self.statementCount,
                statementType: sql.trim().toUpperCase().split(" ")[0],
                executionTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) + 20,
                parseTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 30) + 5,
                databaseTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 150) + 10,
                networkTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 5,
                transactionId: "Txn_" + Date.now(),
                logSequence: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) + 1000,
                redoBytes: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000) + 10000,
                affectedIndexes: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5)
            };
            for (var i = 0; i < Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 1; i++) {
                result.generatedKeys.push({
                    key: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) + 1,
                    column: "ID"
                });
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 30);
    };
    this.beginTransaction = function(callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        this.transactionCount++;
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var transaction = {
                id: "Txn_" + self.transactionCount + "_" + Date.now(),
                sessionId: self.connection.sessionId,
                startTime: Date.now(),
                status: "ACTIVE",
                isolationLevel: "READ_COMMITTED",
                savepoints: [],
                locksAcquired: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) + 1,
                undoSegments: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 1
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, transaction);
        }, 10);
    };
    this.commit = function(callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                status: "COMMITTED",
                transactionId: "Txn_" + self.transactionCount,
                commitTime: Date.now(),
                scn: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000000000) + 1000000000,
                redoBytes: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000) + 10000,
                affectedBlocks: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
                affectedRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 20);
    };
    this.rollback = function(callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                status: "ROLLED_BACK",
                transactionId: "Txn_" + self.transactionCount,
                rollbackTime: Date.now(),
                undoBlocks: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 50,
                affectedRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5000) + 500
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 20);
    };
    this.prepareStatement = function(sql, callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        this.statementCount++;
        var statementId = "Stmt_" + this.statementCount;
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var statement = {
                id: statementId,
                sql: sql,
                parameterCount: (sql.match(/\?/g) || []).length,
                isPoolable: true,
                isClosed: false,
                fetchSize: 100,
                maxFieldSize: 0,
                queryTimeout: 0,
                executionCount: 0,
                totalExecutionTime: 0,
                cacheHitRate: Math.random() * 0.3 + 0.7,
                metadata: {
                    columns: [],
                    parameterMetadata: []
                }
            };
            for (var i = 0; i < statement.parameterCount; i++) {
                statement.metadata.parameterMetadata.push({
                    index: i + 1,
                    type: "VARCHAR2",
                    precision: 255,
                    scale: 0,
                    nullable: true
                });
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, statement);
        }, 10);
    };
    this.getMetaData = function(callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var metadata = {
                driverName: "Oracle JDBC Driver",
                driverVersion: "19.14.0.0",
                databaseName: self.config.database || "ORCL",
                databaseVersion: "Oracle Database 19c Enterprise Edition Release 19.0.0.0.0 - Production",
                serverName: self.config.host || "localhost",
                serverType: "Oracle",
                majorVersion: 19,
                minorVersion: 14,
                jdbcMajorVersion: 19,
                jdbcMinorVersion: 3,
                defaultRowPrefetch: 100,
                defaultBatchValue: 10,
                supportsBatchUpdates: true,
                supportsTransactions: true,
                supportsSavepoints: true,
                supportsStoredProcedures: true,
                supportsUnion: true,
                supportsMultipleTransactions: true,
                supportsMixedCaseIdentifiers: true,
                supportsTableCorrelationNames: true,
                supportsDifferentTableCorrelationNames: true,
                supportsExpressionsInOrderBy: true,
                supportsOrderByUnrelated: true,
                supportsGroupByUnrelated: true,
                supportsGroupByBeyondSelect: true,
                supportsLikeEscapeClause: true,
                supportsMultipleResultSets: true,
                supportsSubqueriesInComparisons: true,
                supportsSubqueriesInExists: true,
                supportsSubqueriesInIns: true,
                supportsCorrelatedSubqueries: true,
                supportsOuterJoins: true,
                supportsFullOuterJoins: true,
                supportsLimitedOuterJoins: true,
                maxConnections: 65536,
                maxStatements: 65535,
                maxStatementLength: 65535,
                maxColumnsInSelect: 1000,
                maxColumnsInTable: 1000,
                maxRows: 0,
                timeout: 0,
                cursorCommitBehavior: 1,
                autoCommitCommitBehavior: 1,
                newProceduresAreCallable: true,
                allProceduresAreCallable: true,
                allTablesAreSelectable: true,
                nonNullableColumnsClustered: false
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, metadata);
        }, 10);
    };
    this.createArrayOf = function(typeName, array, callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                typeName: typeName,
                array: array,
                length: array.length,
                elementType: typeof array[0]
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, 10);
    };
    this.createStruct = function(typeName, attributes, callback) {
        if (!this.connected) {
            if (callback) callback(new Error("Not connected to database"), null);
            return;
        }
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                typeName: typeName,
                attributes: attributes,
                sqlTypeName: typeName,
                javaClassName: "oracle.sql." + typeName.toUpperCase()
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, 10);
    };
};
var OracleSQLBuilder = function() {
    this.tables = {};
    this.currentTable = null;
    this.queryType = null;
    this.columns = [];
    this.values = [];
    this.whereConditions = [];
    this.orderByColumns = [];
    this.groupByColumns = [];
    this.havingConditions = [];
    this.joinClauses = [];
    this.unionQueries = [];
    this.limitValue = null;
    this.offsetValue = null;
    this.set = function(assignments) {
        this.queryType = "UPDATE";
        this.assignments = assignments;
        return this;
    };
    this.into = function(tableName) {
        this.currentTable = tableName;
        if (!this.tables[tableName]) {
            this.tables[tableName] = {
                name: tableName,
                aliases: {},
                columns: {},
                indexes: [],
                constraints: [],
                triggers: []
            };
        }
        return this;
    };
    this.insert = function(tableName) {
        this.queryType = "INSERT";
        this.into(tableName);
        return this;
    };
    this.values = function(valueArray) {
        this.values = valueArray;
        return this;
    };
    this.select = function() {
        this.queryType = "SELECT";
        this.columns = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
        return this;
    };
    this.fromTable = function(tableName, alias) {
        this.currentTable = tableName;
        if (!this.tables[tableName]) {
            this.tables[tableName] = {
                name: tableName,
                alias: alias,
                columns: {},
                schema: "SYSTEM"
            };
        }
        return this;
    };
    this.where = function(condition) {
        this.whereConditions.push(condition);
        return this;
    };
    this.and = function(condition) {
        this.whereConditions.push("AND " + condition);
        return this;
    };
    this.or = function(condition) {
        this.whereConditions.push("OR " + condition);
        return this;
    };
    this.orderBy = function(column, direction) {
        this.orderByColumns.push({ column: column, direction: direction || "ASC" });
        return this;
    };
    this.groupBy = function() {
        this.groupByColumns = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
        return this;
    };
    this.having = function(condition) {
        this.havingConditions.push(condition);
        return this;
    };
    this.join = function(tableName, condition, joinType) {
        joinType = joinType || "INNER";
        this.joinClauses.push({
            table: tableName,
            condition: condition,
            type: joinType
        });
        return this;
    };
    this.leftJoin = function(tableName, condition) {
        return this.join(tableName, condition, "LEFT");
    };
    this.rightJoin = function(tableName, condition) {
        return this.join(tableName, condition, "RIGHT");
    };
    this.fullJoin = function(tableName, condition) {
        return this.join(tableName, condition, "FULL");
    };
    this.crossJoin = function(tableName) {
        return this.join(tableName, "1=1", "CROSS");
    };
    this.union = function(sql) {
        this.unionQueries.push({ sql: sql, type: "UNION" });
        return this;
    };
    this.unionAll = function(sql) {
        this.unionQueries.push({ sql: sql, type: "UNION ALL" });
        return this;
    };
    this.limit = function(value) {
        this.limitValue = value;
        return this;
    };
    this.offset = function(value) {
        this.offsetValue = value;
        return this;
    };
    this.forUpdate = function() {
        this.forUpdateClause = true;
        return this;
    };
    this.nowait = function() {
        this.nowaitClause = true;
        return this;
    };
    this.build = function() {
        var sql = "";
        if (this.queryType === "SELECT") {
            sql = "SELECT ";
            if (this.columns.length === 0) sql += "*";
            else sql += this.columns.join(", ");
            sql += " FROM " + this.currentTable;
            for (var i = 0; i < this.joinClauses.length; i++) {
                var join = this.joinClauses[i];
                sql += " " + join.type + " JOIN " + join.table + " ON " + join.condition;
            }
            if (this.whereConditions.length > 0) {
                sql += " WHERE " + this.whereConditions[0];
                for (var i = 1; i < this.whereConditions.length; i++) {
                    sql += " " + this.whereConditions[i];
                }
            }
            if (this.groupByColumns.length > 0) {
                sql += " GROUP BY " + this.groupByColumns.join(", ");
            }
            if (this.havingConditions.length > 0) {
                sql += " HAVING " + this.havingConditions.join(", ");
            }
            if (this.orderByColumns.length > 0) {
                sql += " ORDER BY ";
                var orders = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < this.orderByColumns.length; i++) {
                    orders.push(this.orderByColumns[i].column + " " + this.orderByColumns[i].direction);
                }
                sql += orders.join(", ");
            }
            if (this.limitValue !== null) {
                sql += " LIMIT " + this.limitValue;
            }
            if (this.offsetValue !== null) {
                sql += " OFFSET " + this.offsetValue;
            }
        } else if (this.queryType === "INSERT") {
            sql = "INSERT INTO " + this.currentTable + " (";
            if (this.columns.length > 0) {
                sql += this.columns.join(", ");
            } else {
                sql += "?".repeat(this.values.length).split("").join(", ");
            }
            sql += ") VALUES (";
            if (this.values.length > 0) {
                var valuePlaceholders = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < this.values.length; i++) {
                    valuePlaceholders.push("?");
                }
                sql += valuePlaceholders.join(", ");
            }
            sql += ")";
        } else if (this.queryType === "UPDATE") {
            sql = "UPDATE " + this.currentTable + " SET ";
            var setClauses = [];
            for (var key in this.assignments) {
                setClauses.push(key + " = ?");
            }
            sql += setClauses.join(", ");
            if (this.whereConditions.length > 0) {
                sql += " WHERE " + this.whereConditions[0];
            }
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < this.unionQueries.length; i++) {
            sql += " " + this.unionQueries[i].type + " " + this.unionQueries[i].sql;
        }
        if (this.forUpdateClause) {
            sql += " FOR UPDATE";
            if (this.nowaitClause) {
                sql += " NOWAIT";
            }
        }
        return sql;
    };
    this.reset = function() {
        this.tables = {};
        this.currentTable = null;
        this.queryType = null;
        this.columns = [];
        this.values = [];
        this.whereConditions = [];
        this.orderByColumns = [];
        this.groupByColumns = [];
        this.havingConditions = [];
        this.joinClauses = [];
        this.unionQueries = [];
        this.limitValue = null;
        this.offsetValue = null;
        this.assignments = {};
        this.forUpdateClause = false;
        this.nowaitClause = false;
    };
};

                deliveryClass: "A",
                dataBrowser: "L",
                appendStructure: null,
                proxyType: null,
                logDataChanges: false,
                maintenanceMode: "S",
                maintenanceType: "S",
                allowedObjectTypes: [],
                activationType: "E"
            },
            descriptors: {
                area: "MEMORY",
                rowsKown: 0,
                hitCount: 0,
                fixedRows: 0,
                createdRows: 0,
                deletedRows: 0,
                modifiedRows: 0,
                currentIndex: 0
            }
        };
        this.tables[tableName] = table;
        return table;
    };
    this.select = function(options) {
        var result = {
            fields: options.fields || [],
            from: options.from,
            where: options.where || [],
            into: options.into,
            clientSpecified: options.clientSpecified || false,
            byPassBuffer: options.byPassBuffer || false,
            directRead: options.directRead || false,
            conditionalExpressions: options.condtab || [],
            withHoldCursor: options.withHoldCursor || false,
            packageSize: options.packageSize || 0,
            upToRows: options.upTo || 0,
            joining: options.joining || null,
            resultSet: [],
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: 0,
            syTabix: 0,
            syFdpos: 0,
            syTleng: 0,
            syTfill: 0,
            syTmaxl: 0
        };
        var numRows = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10;
        for (var i = 0; i < numRows; i++) {
            var row = {};
            for (var j = 0; j < result.fields.length; j++) {
                row[result.fields[j]] = "DATA_" + i + "_" + j;
            }
            result.resultSet.push(row);
        }
        result.syDbcnt = numRows;
        result.syTabix = numRows;
        result.syTfill = numRows;
        return result;
    };
    this.insert = function(tableName, values) {
        var result = {
            table: tableName,
            values: values,
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: 1,
            syTabix: this.tables[tableName] ? this.tables[tableName].rows.length : 0
        };
        if (this.tables[tableName]) {
            this.tables[tableName].rows.push(values);
            this.tables[tableName].descriptors.rowsKown++;
            this.tables[tableName].descriptors.createdRows++;
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.update = function(tableName, values, whereClause) {
        var result = {
            table: tableName,
            values: values,
            where: whereClause,
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) + 1,
            syTabix: 0
        };
        if (this.tables[tableName]) {
            this.tables[tableName].descriptors.modifiedRows += result.syDbcnt;
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.removeRecord = function(tableName, whereClause) {
        var result = {
            table: tableName,
            where: whereClause,
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 1,
            syTabix: 0
        };
        if (this.tables[tableName]) {
            this.tables[tableName].descriptors.deletedRows += result.syDbcnt;
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.modify = function(tableName, values) {
        var result = {
            table: tableName,
            values: values,
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) + 1
        };
        return result;
    };
    this.callFunction = function(functionModuleName, importParameters, changingParameters, exportParameters) {
        var fm = {
            name: functionModuleName,
            programGroup: "STANDARD",
            active: true,
            remoteEnabled: false,
            updateModule: false,
            exceptionClass: "CX_",
            documentation: "Function module documentation",
            importParameters: importParameters || {},
            exportParameters: exportParameters || {},
            changingParameters: changingParameters || {},
            tables: {},
            exceptions: {}
        };
        this.functionModules[functionModuleName] = fm;
        var result = {
            functionModule: functionModuleName,
            callMode: "SYNCHRONOUS",
            executionTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10,
            memoryUsed: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000,
            sySubrc: 0,
            syMsgty: "S",
            syMsgid: "00",
            syMsgno: "001",
            syMsgv1: "",
            syMsgv2: "",
            syMsgv3: "",
            syMsgv4: "",
            exportParameters: fm.exportParameters
        };
        return result;
    };
    this.commitWork = function() {
        return {
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 1,
            syTabix: 0,
            wait: false,
            immediate: false
        };
    };
    this.rollbackWork = function() {
        return {
            sySubrc: 0,
            syDbcnt: 0,
            workProcessesRolledBack: 1
        };
    };
    this.getSystemInfo = function() {
        return this.systemInfo;
    };
    this.setTransactionCode = function(tcode) {
        this.transactionCode = tcode;
        return {
            transactionCode: tcode,
            authorizationCheck: "S",
            svaro: false,
            dynpros: [],
            GUIStatus: null,
            okCode: ""
        };
    };
    this.checkAuthorization = function(object, field, value) {
        return {
            sySubrc: 0,
            authorizationObject: object,
            field: field,
            value: value,
            fieldValue: "S",
            activity: "01"
        };
    };
};

var SAPODataServiceLayer = function(serviceUrl, config) {

    this.config = config || {};
    this.metadata = null;
    this.dataModel = null;
    this.operationGroups = {};
    this.batchOperations = [];
    this.changeSets = [];
    this.deferredGroups = [];

    this.headers = {
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "DataServiceVersion": "2.0",
        "MaxDataServiceVersion": "3.0"
    };
    this.loadMetadata = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            self.metadata = {
                dataServiceVersion: "2.0",
                schemaVersion: {
                    dataServiceVersion: "2.0",
                    schemaVersion: "0000"
                },

                batchUrl: self.serviceUrl + "$batch",
                syncUrl: self.serviceUrl + "$sync"
            };
            for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                self.metadata.entityTypes.push({
                    name: "EntityType_" + (i + 1),
                    namespace: "Namespace" + (i + 1),
                    key: { propertyRef: [{ name: "ID" }] },
                    property: [
                        { name: "ID", type: "Edm.Int32", nullable: false },
                        { name: "Name", type: "Edm.String", maxLength: "100" },
                        { name: "Description", type: "Edm.String", maxLength: "500" },
                        { name: "CreatedAt", type: "Edm.DateTimeOffset" },
                        { name: "ModifiedAt", type: "Edm.DateTimeOffset" },
                        { name: "Status", type: "Edm.String", maxLength: "20" },

                    ],
                    navigationProperty: []
                });
                self.metadata.entitySets.push({
                    name: "EntitySet_" + (i + 1),
                    entityType: "Namespace" + (i + 1) + ".EntityType_" + (i + 1),
                    resultEntityType: "EntityType_" + (i + 1),
                    creatable: true,
                    updatable: true,
                    deletable: true,
                    pageable: true,
                    topable: true,
                    countable: true,
                    sortable: true,
                    filterable: true
                });
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                self.metadata.functionImports.push({
                    name: "FunctionImport_" + (i + 1),
                    httpMethod: "POST",
                    returnType: "Edm.String",
                    entitySet: "EntitySet_1",
                    parameter: [
                        { name: "Param1", type: "Edm.String", mode: "In" },
                        { name: "Param2", type: "Edm.Int32", mode: "In" }
                    ],
                    sideEffect: true
                });
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, self.metadata);
        }, 100);
    };
    this.read = function(entitySet, options, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                results: [],
                __count: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
                __batchResults: null,
                d: {
                    results: [],
                    __deferred: {
                        uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet
                    }
                },
                statusCode: 200,
                headers: self.headers,
                etag: "W/\"datetime'" + new Date().toISOString() + "'\"",
                requestUri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet,
                dataPath: entitySet
            };
            var numResults = options.$top || options.top || Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10;
            for (var i = 0; i < numResults; i++) {
                var entity = {
                    __metadata: {
                        uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + i + ")",
                        type: "Namespace1.EntityType_1",
                        editUrl: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + i + ")",
                        mediaSrc: null,
                        contentType: null
                    },
                    ID: i,
                    Name: "Entity_" + i,
                    Description: "Description for entity " + i,
                    CreatedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                    ModifiedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                    Status: ["Active", "Inactive", "Pending"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)],
                    Category: ["CategoryA", "CategoryB", "CategoryC"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)]
                };
                result.results.push(entity);
                result.d.results.push(entity);
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) + 100);
    };
    this.create = function(entitySet, data, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                __metadata: {
                    uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + ")",
                    type: "Namespace1.EntityType_1"
                },
                ID: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1,
                Name: data.Name || "NewEntity",
                Description: data.Description || "",
                CreatedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                ModifiedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                Status: "Active",
                Category: data.Category || "CategoryA"
            };
            result.__batchResults = [result];
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 150) + 50);
    };
    this.update = function(entitySet, id, data, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                __metadata: {
                    uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + id + ")",
                    type: "Namespace1.EntityType_1"
                },
                ID: id,
                Name: data.Name,
                Description: data.Description,
                ModifiedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                Status: data.Status,
                Category: data.Category
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 50);
    };
    this.remove = function(entitySet, id, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                response: {
                    statusCode: 204,
                    statusText: "No Content",
                    headers: {
                        "odata-version": "2.0",
                        "cache-control": "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate"
                    }
                }
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 30);
    };
    this.callFunction = function(functionName, method, params, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                d: {
                    __metadata: {
                        type: "Namespace1.FunctionImport_" + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 1)
                    },
                    Result: "Function call successful",
                    OutputParam1: "Value_" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
                    OutputParam2: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)
                }
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) + 100);
    };
    this.executeBatch = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                __batchResponses: [],
                __changeResponses: [],
                response: {
                    statusCode: 200,
                    statusText: "OK",
                    headers: {
                        "odata-version": "2.0",
                        "content-type": "multipart/mixed; boundary=batch" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2)
                    }
                }
            };
            for (var i = 0; i < self.batchOperations.length; i++) {
                result.__batchResponses.push({
                    statusCode: 200,
                    headers: {},
                    body: { d: { results: [] } }
                });
            }
            self.batchOperations = [];
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 300) + 150);
    };
    this.addBatchOperation = function(operation) {
        this.batchOperations.push(operation);
    };
    this.createChangeSet = function() {
        var changeSet = {
            id: "CS_" + Date.now(),
            operations: []
        };
        this.changeSets.push(changeSet);
        return changeSet;
    };
    this.getXcsrfToken = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            self.xcsrfToken = "xCSRF-" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15) + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15);
            self.headers["x-csrf-token"] = self.xcsrfToken;
            if (callback) callback(null, { token: self.xcsrfToken });
        }, 50);
    };
};

    this.lookup = function(jndiName) {
        var result = {
            __ref__: jndiName,
            __class__: "javax.naming.InitialContext",
            __data__: {}
        };
        if (this.jndiContext[jndiName]) {
            return this.jndiContext[jndiName];
        }
        var obj = {
            jndiName: jndiName,
            className: "Unknown",
            properties: {}
        };
        if (jndiName.indexOf("jdbc/") === 0) {
            obj.className = "javax.sql.DataSource";
            obj.properties = {
                URL: "jdbc:oracle:thin:@localhost:1521:ORCL",
                user: "system",
                password: "******",
                maxPoolSize: 50,
                minPoolSize: 5
            };
        } else if (jndiName.indexOf("jms/") === 0) {
            obj.className = "javax.jms.ConnectionFactory";
            obj.properties = {
                queueManager: "QMGR01",
                channel: "SYSTEM.DEF.SVRCONN",
                host: "localhost",
                port: 1414
            };
        } else if (jndiName.indexOf("mail/") === 0) {
            obj.className = "javax.mail.Session";
            obj.properties = {
                host: "smtp.company.com",
                port: 25
            };
        }
        this.jndiContext[jndiName] = obj;
        return obj;
    };
    this.getInitialContext = function() {
        if (!this.initialContext) {
            this.initialContext = {
                environment: {
                    "java.naming.factory.initial": "com.ibm.websphere.naming.WsnInitialContextFactory",
                    "java.naming.provider.url": "corbaloc:iiop:localhost:2809",
                    "java.naming.factory.url.pkgs": "com.ibm.websphere.naming",

                },

                    name: "",

                },
                lookup: this.lookup.bind(this)
            };
        }
        return this.initialContext;
    };
    this.startMessageListener = function(config) {
        var listener = {
            id: "Listener_" + Date.now(),

            clientId: config.clientId || null,
            state: "STARTED",
            messagesReceived: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000,
            messagesProcessed: Math.floor(Math.random() * 9000) + 900,
            failedMessages: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 1,
            averageProcessingTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
            startTime: Date.now() - Math.floor(Math.random() * 86400000),
            lastMessageTime: Date.now() - Math.floor(Math.random() * 3600000)
        };
        this.messageListeners.push(listener);
        return listener;
    };
    this.getServerInfo = function() {
        return {
            nodeName: this.nodeName,
            cellName: this.cellName,
            serverName: this.serverName,
            clusterName: this.clusterName,
            serverType: "APPLICATION_SERVER",
            version: this.version,
            platform: "Distributed",
            operatingSystem: require("os").platform(),
            operatingSystemVersion: require("os").release(),
            architecture: require("os").arch(),
            cpuCount: require("os").cpus().length,
            totalMemory: Math.round(require("os").totalmem() / 1073741824) + " GB",
            serverGroup: null,
            cells: this.cellName,
            nodes: this.nodeName,
            applicationServers: [this.serverName]
        };
    };
    this.createServiceReference = function(serviceName, interfaceName, implementation) {
        this.serviceReferences[serviceName] = {
            serviceName: serviceName,
            interfaceName: interfaceName,
            implementation: implementation,
            properties: {},
            references: []
        };
        return this.serviceReferences[serviceName];
    };
    this.getResourceReference = function(resourceName) {
        if (!this.resourceReferences[resourceName]) {
            this.resourceReferences[resourceName] = {
                jndiName: resourceName,
                type: "Unknown",
                authentication: "Container",
                sharingScope: "Shareable",
                resAuth: "CONTAINER",
                resSharingScope: "Shareable"
            };
        }
        return this.resourceReferences[resourceName];
    };

var MicrosoftDynamicsAX = function(config) {
    this.aosInstance = config.aosInstance || "AOS01";
    this.companyId = config.companyId || "USMF";
    this.languageId = config.languageId || "en-us";
    this.userId = config.userId || "Admin";
    this.partition = config.partition || "DAT";
    this.partitionKey = config.partitionKey || "DAT";
    this.ttsLevel = 0;
    this.connid = null;
    this.sessionId = null;
    this.queryResults = {};
    this.dataSources = {};
    this.queryRun = null;
    this.queryBuild = null;
    this.args = null;
    this.caller = null;
    this.infolog = [];
    this.sysGlobalObjectCache = {};
    this.tableBufferPool = {};
    this.queryStatistics = {
        totalQueries: 0,
        cachedQueries: 0,
        fullTableScans: 0,
        indexLookups: 0,
        averageExecutionTime: 0,
        slowestQuery: null,
        mostFrequentQuery: null
    };
