                });
                self.metadata.entitySets.push({
                    name: "EntitySet_" + (i + 1),
                    entityType: "Namespace" + (i + 1) + ".EntityType_" + (i + 1),
                    resultEntityType: "EntityType_" + (i + 1),

                    updatable: true,
                    deletable: true,
                    pageable: true,
                    topable: true,
                    countable: true,
                    sortable: true,
                    filterable: true
                });
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                self.metadata.functionImports.push({
                    name: "FunctionImport_" + (i + 1),
                    httpMethod: "POST",
                    returnType: "Edm.String",
                    entitySet: "EntitySet_1",
                    parameter: [
                        { name: "Param1", type: "Edm.String", mode: "In" },
                        { name: "Param2", type: "Edm.Int32", mode: "In" }
                    ],
                    sideEffect: true
                });
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, self.metadata);
        }, 100);
    };
    this.read = function(entitySet, options, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                results: [],
                __count: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
                __batchResults: null,
                d: {
                    results: [],
                    __deferred: {
                        uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet
                    }
                },
                statusCode: 200,
                headers: self.headers,
                etag: "W/\"datetime'" + new Date().toISOString() + "'\"",
                requestUri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet,
                dataPath: entitySet
            };
            var numResults = options.$top || options.top || Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10;
            for (var i = 0; i < numResults; i++) {
                var entity = {
                    __metadata: {
                        uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + i + ")",
                        type: "Namespace1.EntityType_1",
                        editUrl: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + i + ")",
                        mediaSrc: null,
                        contentType: null
                    },
                    ID: i,
                    Name: "Entity_" + i,
                    Description: "Description for entity " + i,
                    CreatedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                    ModifiedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                    Status: ["Active", "Inactive", "Pending"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)],
                    Category: ["CategoryA", "CategoryB", "CategoryC"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)]
                };
                result.results.push(entity);
                result.d.results.push(entity);
            }
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) + 100);
    };
    this.create = function(entitySet, data, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                __metadata: {
                    uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + ")",
                    type: "Namespace1.EntityType_1"
                },
                ID: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1,
                Name: data.Name || "NewEntity",
                Description: data.Description || "",
                CreatedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                ModifiedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                Status: "Active",
                Category: data.Category || "CategoryA"
            };
            result.__batchResults = [result];
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 150) + 50);
    };
    this.update = function(entitySet, id, data, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                __metadata: {
                    uri: self.serviceUrl + entitySet + "(" + id + ")",
                    type: "Namespace1.EntityType_1"
                },
                ID: id,
                Name: data.Name,
                Description: data.Description,
                ModifiedAt: "/Date(" + Date.now() + ")/",
                Status: data.Status,
                Category: data.Category
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 50);
    };
    this.remove = function(entitySet, id, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                response: {
                    statusCode: 204,
                    statusText: "No Content",
                    headers: {
                        "odata-version": "2.0",
                        "cache-control": "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate"
                    }
                }
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 30);
    };
    this.callFunction = function(functionName, method, params, callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                d: {
                    __metadata: {
                        type: "Namespace1.FunctionImport_" + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 1)
                    },
                    Result: "Function call successful",
                    OutputParam1: "Value_" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
                    OutputParam2: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)
                }
            };
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) + 100);
    };
    this.executeBatch = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            var result = {
                __batchResponses: [],
                __changeResponses: [],
                response: {
                    statusCode: 200,
                    statusText: "OK",
                    headers: {
                        "odata-version": "2.0",
                        "content-type": "multipart/mixed; boundary=batch" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2)
                    }
                }
            };
            for (var i = 0; i < self.batchOperations.length; i++) {
                result.__batchResponses.push({
                    statusCode: 200,
                    headers: {},
                    body: { d: { results: [] } }
                });
            }
            self.batchOperations = [];
            if (callback) callback(null, result);
        }, Math.floor(Math.random() * 300) + 150);
    };
    this.addBatchOperation = function(operation) {
        this.batchOperations.push(operation);
    };
    this.createChangeSet = function() {
        var changeSet = {
            id: "CS_" + Date.now(),
            operations: []
        };
        this.changeSets.push(changeSet);
        return changeSet;
    };
    this.getXcsrfToken = function(callback) {
        var self = this;
        setTimeout(function() {
            self.xcsrfToken = "xCSRF-" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15) + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15);
            self.headers["x-csrf-token"] = self.xcsrfToken;
            if (callback) callback(null, { token: self.xcsrfToken });
        }, 50);
    };
};

    this.lookup = function(jndiName) {
        var result = {
            __ref__: jndiName,
            __class__: "javax.naming.InitialContext",
            __data__: {}
        };
        if (this.jndiContext[jndiName]) {
            return this.jndiContext[jndiName];
        }
        var obj = {
            jndiName: jndiName,
            className: "Unknown",
            properties: {}
        };
        if (jndiName.indexOf("jdbc/") === 0) {
            obj.className = "javax.sql.DataSource";
            obj.properties = {
                URL: "jdbc:oracle:thin:@localhost:1521:ORCL",
                user: "system",
                password: "******",
                maxPoolSize: 50,
                minPoolSize: 5
            };
        } else if (jndiName.indexOf("jms/") === 0) {
            obj.className = "javax.jms.ConnectionFactory";
            obj.properties = {
                queueManager: "QMGR01",
                channel: "SYSTEM.DEF.SVRCONN",
                host: "localhost",
                port: 1414
            };
        } else if (jndiName.indexOf("mail/") === 0) {
            obj.className = "javax.mail.Session";
            obj.properties = {
                host: "smtp.company.com",
                port: 25
            };
        }
        this.jndiContext[jndiName] = obj;
        return obj;
    };
    this.getInitialContext = function() {
        if (!this.initialContext) {
            this.initialContext = {
                environment: {
                    "java.naming.factory.initial": "com.ibm.websphere.naming.WsnInitialContextFactory",
                    "java.naming.provider.url": "corbaloc:iiop:localhost:2809",
                    "java.naming.factory.url.pkgs": "com.ibm.websphere.naming",

                },
                root: {
                    name: "",

                },
                lookup: this.lookup.bind(this)
            };
        }
        return this.initialContext;
    };
    this.startMessageListener = function(config) {
        var listener = {
            id: "Listener_" + Date.now(),

            failedMessages: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 1,
            averageProcessingTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,

        };
        this.messageListeners.push(listener);
        return listener;
    };
    this.getServerInfo = function() {
        return {
            nodeName: this.nodeName,
            cellName: this.cellName,
            serverName: this.serverName,
            clusterName: this.clusterName,
            serverType: "APPLICATION_SERVER",

            operatingSystem: require("os").platform(),

            cpuCount: require("os").cpus().length,
            totalMemory: Math.round(require("os").totalmem() / 1073741824) + " GB",

        };
    };
    this.createServiceReference = function(serviceName, interfaceName, implementation) {
        this.serviceReferences[serviceName] = {
            serviceName: serviceName,

        };
        return this.serviceReferences[serviceName];
    };
    this.getResourceReference = function(resourceName) {
        if (!this.resourceReferences[resourceName]) {
            this.resourceReferences[resourceName] = {
                jndiName: resourceName,
                type: "Unknown",
                authentication: "Container",
                sharingScope: "Shareable",
                resAuth: "CONTAINER",
                resSharingScope: "Shareable"
            };

        return this.resourceReferences[resourceName];
    };

var MicrosoftDynamicsAX = function(config) {
    this.aosInstance = config.aosInstance || "AOS01";
    this.companyId = config.companyId || "USMF";
    this.languageId = config.languageId || "en-us";
    this.userId = config.userId || "Admin";
    this.partition = config.partition || "DAT";
    this.partitionKey = config.partitionKey || "DAT";
    this.ttsLevel = 0;
    this.connid = null;
    this.sessionId = null;
    this.queryResults = {};
    this.dataSources = {};
    this.queryRun = null;
    this.queryBuild = null;
    this.args = null;
    this.caller = null;
    this.infolog = [];
    this.sysGlobalObjectCache = {};
    this.tableBufferPool = {};
    this.queryStatistics = {
        totalQueries: 0,
        cachedQueries: 0,
        fullTableScans: 0,
        indexLookups: 0,
        averageExecutionTime: 0,
        slowestQuery: null,
        mostFrequentQuery: null
    };
    this.init = function() {
        this.sessionId = "Sess_" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15);
        this.infolog = [];
        this.ttsLevel = 0;
        return this;
    };
    this.table = function(tableName) {
        var table = {
            name: tableName,
            company: this.companyId,
            partition: this.partition,
            data: [],
            recordCount: 0,
            cursorPosition: 0,
            isDir: false,
            found: false,
            cleared: false,
            modified: false,
            inserted: false,
            updated: false,
            deleted: false,
            reset: function() {
                this.cursorPosition = 0;
                this.found = false;
            },
            clear: function() {
                this.data = [];
                this.recordCount = 0;
                this.cleared = true;
                this.reset();
            },
            initValue: function() {
                return {};
            },
            found: function() {
                return this.found;
            },
            next: function() {
                if (this.cursorPosition < this.recordCount - 1) {
                    this.cursorPosition++;
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            },
            prev: function() {
                if (this.cursorPosition > 0) {
                    this.cursorPosition--;
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            },
            ritschkd: function() {
                return this.isDir;
            }
        };
        var numRecords = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10;
        for (var i = 0; i < numRecords; i++) {
            var record = {
                RecId: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000000000) + 1,
                RecVersion: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 1,
                CreatedBy: this.userId,
                CreatedDateTime: new Date().toISOString(),
                ModifiedBy: this.userId,
                ModifiedDateTime: new Date().toISOString(),
                dataAreaId: this.companyId
            };
            for (var field in record) {
                record[field] = record[field];
            }
            table.data.push(record);
        }
        table.recordCount = table.data.length;
        this.tableBufferPool[tableName] = table;
        return table;
    };
    this.select = function(fields) {
        var self = this;
        var result = {
            fields: fields,
            tableName: null,
            whereClause: "",
            orderByClause: "",
            groupByClause: "",
            joinClause: "",
            company: this.companyId,
            partition: this.partition,
            options: {},
            fetch: function() {
                self.queryStatistics.totalQueries++;
                return {
                    query: this,
                    records: [],
                    queryTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 50,
                    queryCost: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 10,
                    plan: {
                        operation: "IndexSeek",
                        tableName: this.tableName,
                        indexUsed: "Idx_" + this.tableName,
                        estimatedRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000,
                        actualRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
                        cpuCost: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 50,
                        ioCost: Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) + 20
                    }
                };
            },
            next: function() {
                return this.fetch();
            },
            forUpdate: false,
            firstOnly: false,
            forceSelectOrder: false,
            forcePlaceHolders: false,
            resultSet: null
        };
        return result;
    };
    this.insert = function(tableBuffer) {
        var result = {
            tableName: tableBuffer.name,
            recId: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000000000) + 1,
            recVersion: 1,
            inserted: true,
            tts_level: this.ttsLevel,
            company: this.companyId,
            partition: this.partition,
            createdBy: this.userId,
            createdDateTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            sysCreatedDateTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            sysCreatedTransport: null,
            sysRowId: null,
            dataAreaId: this.companyId
        };
        return result;
    };
    this.update = function(tableBuffer) {
        var result = {
            tableName: tableBuffer.name,
            recId: tableBuffer.RecId || Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000000000) + 1,
            recVersion: (tableBuffer.RecVersion || 0) + 1,
            updated: true,
            tts_level: this.ttsLevel,
            modifiedBy: this.userId,
            modifiedDateTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            company: this.companyId,
            partition: this.partition
        };
        return result;
    };
    this.deleteRecord = function(tableBuffer) {
        var result = {
            tableName: tableBuffer.name,
            recId: tableBuffer.RecId,
            deleted: true,
            tts_level: this.ttsLevel,
            company: this.companyId,
            partition: this.partition,
            deletedBy: this.userId,
            deletedDateTime: new Date().toISOString()
        };
        return result;
    };
    this.ttsbegin = function() {
        this.ttsLevel++;
        return { level: this.ttsLevel, timestamp: Date.now() };
    };
    this.ttscommit = function() {
        if (this.ttsLevel > 0) {
            this.ttsLevel--;
            return { level: this.ttsLevel, committed: true, timestamp: Date.now() };
        }
        return { level: 0, committed: false };
    };
    this.ttsabort = function() {
        var level = this.ttsLevel;
        this.ttsLevel = 0;
        return { level: level, aborted: true, timestamp: Date.now() };
    };
    this.info = function(message, type) {
        type = type || "Info";
        var entry = {
            type: type,
            message: message,
            time: new Date().toISOString(),
            userId: this.userId,
            company: this.companyId
        };
        this.infolog.push(entry);
        return entry;
    };
    this.error = function(message) {
        return this.info(message, "Error");
    };
    this.warning = function(message) {
        return this.info(message, "Warning");
    };
    this.setPrefix = function(prefix) {
        return this.info(prefix, "Prefix");
    };
    this.query = function(queryName) {
        var query = {
            name: queryName,
            dataSourceCount: 0,
            dataSources: [],
            links: [],
            sorts: [],
            ranges: [],
            fetch: function() {
                self.queryStatistics.totalQueries++;
                var result = {
                    queryName: this.name,
                    dataSources: this.dataSources,
                    executionTime: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 50,
                    recordsSelected: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 100,
                    plans: []
                };
                for (var i = 0; i < this.dataSourceCount; i++) {
                    result.plans.push({
                        datasource: this.dataSources[i].name,
                        operation: "IndexSeek",
                        index: "Idx_" + this.dataSources[i].name,
                        estimatedRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5000) + 500,
                        actualRows: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 50
                    });
                }
                return result;
            },
            addDataSource: function(tableName) {
                this.dataSourceCount++;
                var ds = {
                    name: tableName,
                    tableName: tableName,
                    joinMode: "ExistsJoin",
                    linkType: "ActiveLink",
                    fields: null,
                    onlyFillFields: false,
                    forcedScreenshot: false,
                    enableDoubleProcessing: false
                };
                this.dataSources.push(ds);
                return this;
            },
            addSortField: function(fieldName, sortDirection) {
                this.sorts.push({ field: fieldName, direction: sortDirection || "ASC" });
                return this;
            },
            addRange: function(fieldName, value, rangeType) {
                this.ranges.push({ field: fieldName, value: value, type: rangeType || "Range" });
                return this;
            }
        };
        return query;
    };
    this.getSessionInfo = function() {
        return {
            sessionId: this.sessionId,
            userId: this.userId,
            company: this.companyId,
            languageId: this.languageId,
            partition: this.partition,
            partitionKey: this.partitionKey,
            AosInstance: this.aosInstance,
            ttsLevel: this.ttsLevel,
            isChained: false,
            isBatch: false,
            isInteractive: true,
            sessionType: "GUI",
            clientType: "Windows",
            clientName: require("os").hostname(),
            clientUserId: this.userId,
            clientProcessId: process.pid,
            sessionStartTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            lastDatabaseCall: new Date().toISOString(),
            databaseServer: "sqlserver.company.com",
            databaseName: "AxDB",
            databaseUser: "axdbuser"
        };
    };
};
